# Graph Report - solid-pancake  (2026-08-03)

## Corpus Check
- 751 files · ~7,557,922 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 9350 nodes · 10747 edges · 879 communities (801 shown, 78 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 62 edges (avg confidence: 0.79)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `17c633fc`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
